As is tradition, Friday was the last chance for players to earn two byes for Grand Prix Rimini. Their last chance and our first, that was, to get an early impression of what we should expect to see at the top tables over the course of the weekend. All of the following decks were piloted to perfect 5-0 records in yesterday's 32-player single-elimination Trials. Check out what the succesful players chose to run, now, in week one after Pro Tour *Eldritch Moon* ... 

#### Maxime Martin, Grand Prix Trial Winner

##### 

 ![](https://magic.wizards.com/sites/all/modules/features/wiz_bean_content_deck_list/icons/decklist_download.png)
